## Horizontal MCU invariant
The converter callbacks receive curX * state->stepX (api/decapi.c:125), and Setsize accepts only scales 1, 2, 4, 8 (jpegdec/jdec.c:11). Native MCU widths in these three formats are 32, 16, 16; after any accepted scale, those steps remain multiples of the corresponding horizontal chroma groups 4, 2, 2. Thus each non-empty row computes its chroma contributions before using them. The target has no default-zero contributions; its first grouped column initializes them. SDK assertions record this internal callback precondition; ASSERTLINE expands to a check with DEBUG and to ((void)0) without DEBUG in the 43U release build. This is a source contract, not reliance on an undefined value for a valid decoder call.
The local MSL assert.h is a zero-argument stub and rejected the contract expression. The standard SDK ASSERTLINE(condition, line) is used instead; DEBUG builds check the condition and the 43U release build omits it. No shared header was edited.

## Horizontal MCU invariant
The converter callbacks receive curX * state->stepX (api/decapi.c:125), and Setsize accepts only scales 1, 2, 4, 8 (jpegdec/jdec.c:11). Native MCU widths in these three formats are 32, 16, 16; after any accepted scale, those steps remain multiples of the corresponding horizontal chroma groups 4, 2, 2. Thus each non-empty row computes its chroma contributions before using them. The target has no default-zero contributions; its first grouped column initializes them. SDK assertions record this internal callback precondition; ASSERTLINE expands to a check with DEBUG and to ((void)0) without DEBUG in the 43U release build. This is a source contract, not reliance on an undefined value for a valid decoder call.

Store temporary experiment completed modes 411/422/420/211 and 444 normal; 444 edge has its existing reverse green/blue sum and did not match the script template. No failing source retained. Only 411 edge improved, with blue input reuse and separate masked channel values. Restored normal source line formatting after the alias experiment.
native edge callback equivalence: 149040 valid MCU cases PASS; address/undefined sanitizers enabled
The native fixture preserves the real field types and 0x184-byte MCU buffer; old/new callback bodies are compiled directly from the saved C and live C. All supported scales, zero/full/partial border extents, x/y MCU origins, both border branches and randomized decoded blocks are compared, with debug assertions active. This checks valid-call equivalence, not PPC instruction matching.

native edge callback equivalence: 151200 valid MCU cases PASS; address/undefined sanitizers enabled
The native fixture preserves the real field types and 0x184-byte MCU buffer; old/new callback bodies are compiled directly from the saved C and live C. All supported scales, zero/full/partial border extents, x/y MCU origins, both border branches and randomized decoded blocks are compared, with debug assertions active. This checks valid-call equivalence, not PPC instruction matching.
Additional scope/return experiments: IDCT Col has separate odd-difference declarations, so the initial scope parser was adjusted before its three actual row/column/both scope variants; all three were measured and restored. Aggregate Huffman limit initializers were rejected by MWCC; the saved valid helper was restored. Named result, byte index, reader ordering, explicit inline and const fast-result variants produced unchanged stack slots and were restored. The 411 row decomposition template completed four RGBA variants with no retained change; RGB already puts the tile product first and did not match that template, so no RGB edit was made.
Final native callback check covers both full and edge converters in all five color modes: 151200 valid MCU cases passed with address and undefined-behavior sanitizers. Only supported scales and caller-aligned MCU origins are used. All originally exact grayscale bodies and the newly exact RGB565 444 edge body remain preserved.

## final function audit after full gate

All open functions have at least five distinct source-level variants recorded above. Counts agree for every decoder/converter body; both setters remain one instruction short. Remaining differences include scheduling and Huffman stack slots, so these units are partial matches. The new exact function is RGB565 444 edge, confirmed again after the clean build with 104/104 instructions and ctxdiff diffs 0. No allocated data exists in these four objects; data bytes remain 0 -> 0.

Changed C units are Texture_MCUtoRGBA8.c, Texture_MCUtoRGB565.c, idct_block_var.c and iqdec_b65_frv32.c. Only these four units and the attempt/gate records were changed. Final full gate passed with zero regressions, zero added forbidden patterns and zero readability warnings. Matching flags remain unchanged. No semantic uncertainty was observed in the converter checks; IDCT/entropy evidence is from target assembly, instruction diffs and objdiff.
Removed the now-unused first-pass iter initializer after conversion to a pointer/count loop. Both IDCT function bodies are byte-identical to the preceding gated build; iter remains the live column-pass loop counter.
